- [2026-07-02 01:01] result/diagnostic: R147 Mask2Former logits/threshold diagnostic completed on remote GPU0 and artifacts were synced locally. R146 checkpoint foreground class scores were high (`~0.922`, all 48 queries kept), but mask union probabilities were collapsed to `1e-5` scale: train mean/max union max `1.30e-05/5.20e-05`, val `9.65e-06/1.31e-05`, clean-test-v2 `1.03e-05/1.11e-05`. Thresholds from `0.001` to `0.35` produced empty masks and Dice `0` on train/val/clean. This proves the empty-mask failure is not caused by class-score gating; the learned mask logits themselves are extremely negative.
- [2026-07-02 01:05] result/diagnostic: R148 low-probability rank diagnostic completed on remote GPU0 and artifacts were synced locally. Even when probing thresholds down to `1e-6`, best fixed-threshold Dice was only train `0.148271`, val `0.085552`, clean-test-v2 `0.188526`. A GT-area top-k oracle, used only as a non-deployable spatial-rank diagnostic, reached only train `0.355161`, val `0.239494`, clean-test-v2 `0.268285`, with Boundary IoU around `0.01`. Conclusion: R145/R146 did not learn useful spatial mask logits, not merely miscalibrated probabilities. Do not run a full HF Mask2Former training job or threshold/readout sweep from this checkpoint. Next useful step is R149 target/loss wiring rescue: force a 1-2 image overfit with corrected target formatting/loss/readout checks, and abandon the HF path if that cannot recover high train Dice.
- [2026-07-02 01:15] result/diagnostic: R149 union-target HF Mask2Former 1-image overfit rescue completed on remote GPU0 and artifacts were synced locally. Switching the target from many instance masks to a single binary union mask restored mask probability scale: train union max reached `1.0` after 300 steps. Best train Dice reached `0.811855` at threshold `0.5`, with Precision `0.765057`, Recall `0.864752`, Boundary IoU `0.121593`; however this failed the predeclared overfit gate `>=0.85`. Val Dice was `0.017860` and clean-test-v2 diagnostic Dice was `0.303562`, both not quality claims. Interpretation: the HF model can learn a binary union target, so the R146 all-empty failure is not a broken dependency/model, but the tiny configuration still does not overfit cleanly enough for a full run.

- [2026-07-06] audit: R200 compared R110 against the trained ARAA DANet epoch-98 checkpoint using the same clean-test-v2 masks and bridge/boundary/component metric script. R110 beats ARAA on Dice `0.917723` vs `0.911766`, IoU `0.848570` vs `0.838436`, Boundary IoU `0.251896` vs `0.235265`, and Boundary F1 `0.394721` vs `0.374350`, but is worse on false-bridge `0.790123` vs `0.679012` and component-count error `2.506173` vs `2.086420`. Conclusion: R110 improves overlap/boundary quality but does not yet solve bone-gap adhesion/component separation relative to ARAA. Full audit: `outputs/analysis/r200_r110_vs_araa_bridge_boundary_audit.json`; report: `research-workflow/refine-logs/R200_R110_VS_ARAA_CONSTRAINT_AUDIT.md`.

- [2026-07-14 22:28] R258 OOF training restarted safely after rejecting the first full run for a fold-4 epoch-1 `Infinity` loss. Remote synthetic tests confirmed finite extreme-logit loss for float16 and float32 inputs.
